# Changelog

All notable changes to Halvo are recorded in this file.

The format follows [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/), and
the project uses [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

To record a change, add one line at the end of the list under the Unreleased
heading: `- <Type>: <text>`, where Type is Added, Changed, Deprecated, Removed,
Fixed or Security. A pull request adds one entry for each type of change it
makes. The list has no `###` headings, so that two branches can each add a line
and merge without an entry landing under the heading of another.
`sh scripts/check-changelog.sh` checks the file.

## [Unreleased]

- Added: Document the build command and record it failing on the empty repository
- Added: Ignore build output, node_modules and Claude worktrees
- Added: Add the Tauri 2 app that shows and prints its version
- Added: Add the GitHub Actions workflow that builds both platforms
- Added: Add the changelog and the release dry run
- Added: Add the ADRs for the three scaffold decisions
- Added: Copy the research reports and the plan into docs
- Added: Build the bare executable on Windows without installer tooling
- Added: Update the plan copy after the ledger plan revisions
- Added: Refresh the research report copies after the review fixes
- Added: Add the R1 gate harness and the pre-crate baseline
- Added: Scaffold the halvo-capture crate with shared types and module boundaries
- Added: Silence the shellcheck findings in the gate harness
- Added: Run cargo test, clippy and fmt on both CI jobs
- Added: Add the consent status types, stored-data inventory, pause and storage cap with mock-store tests
- Added: Add just-in-time macOS permission onboarding with System Settings deep links and lost-grant walk-back
- Added: Add the data view, its Tauri commands and the menu-bar capture indicator
- Added: Add failing tests for the adversarial review of the encrypted store
- Added: Measure the peak resident set of a 512 MiB segment write and read in an ignored test
- Added: Prove the store crypto-shred and single-writer invariants over random operation sequences and process races
- Added: Add the macOS secure-input guard and the capture backend dependencies
- Added: Add the idempotent macOS LaunchAgent install for the capture daemon
- Added: Add the macOS capture sources for screen, keystroke, pointer, foreground app and window title
- Added: Add the Windows capture dependencies for Graphics Capture, Raw Input and the registry
- Added: Add the Windows capture backend behind cfg with the logon-task install and unit tests
- Added: Add a gate lint banning low-level input hooks in the capture crate
- Added: Reject attribution lines in commit messages with a commit-msg hook and a CI step
- Added: Add AGENTS.md with the rule table and import it from CLAUDE.md
- Added: Add failing fixtures for a trailer below scissors text that git keeps
- Added: Add failing fixtures for scissors lines git does not write and for a merge conclusion
- Changed: Split the foreground state into one writer and readers and name the unpersisted placement
- Changed: Make the secure-input zero-keystroke invariant a type and a test
- Changed: Make the secure-input hold RAII and name the screen self-exclusion step
- Changed: Own window-title CoreFoundation values by Drop instead of manual CFRelease
- Changed: Subtract the unwired audio and mic on/off and name the XML, installer and start-failure types
- Changed: Make the inventory sorted by construction so eviction shreds the oldest day
- Changed: Request the Accessibility grant through the system prompt and name request outcomes by what the code knows
- Changed: Keep one public source for the pane title the running macOS shows
- Changed: Derive the grants to record from the permission list instead of a duplicate field
- Changed: Resolve the consent settings path through Tauri so the identifier lives only in tauri.conf.json
- Changed: Write the storage cap constants from the window, the daily estimate and the headroom
- Changed: Accept a platform refusal of a contending writer in the single-writer race check
- Changed: Name the calendar constants and the year-month-day conversions of UtcDay
- Changed: Add the Windows crate features for the new input, HID and registry-watch constants
- Changed: Merge CHANGELOG.md with git's union driver so parallel entries stop conflicting
- Changed: Print the help of the commit-message check from here-documents
- Changed: Name the newest exempt commit and record why it is exempt in AGENTS.md
- Changed: Name the parameters of expect and the one-mode rule of the option parser
- Changed: Say in the CI log why the commit-message step checks every commit
- Changed: Say newest exempt commit where the range check said baseline
- Fixed: Read Windows raw input through an aligned buffer and close the pump start/stop race, carrying decoded payload sizes to an explicit metadata-only placement
- Fixed: Watch every registry root and path the installed-software scan reads and parse SystemComponent to a bool at the boundary
- Fixed: Quote every scheduled-task argument token and match the Task Scheduler Command element
- Fixed: Drop only the diff below git's own scissors block from the hook file
- Fixed: Report a failed awk as an error instead of an attribution line
- Fixed: Name the work-tree-root test of prerequisites.sh and make the name true
- Fixed: Match the scissors line by git's comment string and keep empty lines in its block
- Fixed: Say in the CI echo that exempt commits are not checked
- Fixed: Clamp a storage cap read from consent.json to the minimum so a zero cap cannot shred the store
- Added: Add SQLCipher and AEAD dependencies for the encrypted segment store
- Added: Add dev-fallback and release key custody for the store key
- Added: Add the encrypted segment store, crypto-shred and verify paths
- Changed: Strip comments from the encrypted store and custody files
- Changed: Build SQLCipher with its libtomcrypt provider instead of OpenSSL
- Changed: Stream segment files through bounded chunks instead of buffering them
- Fixed: Propagate keychain errors and bind each custody to a RootId
- Changed: Make the store a single writer with retention as a parameter and a sealed state
- Changed: Check deletion by decrypting with every remaining key
- Fixed: Destroy the keychain roots the store tests create when each test ends
- Fixed: Hold the store under an exclusive database lock and complete every shred before reporting it
- Fixed: Make the store and deletion gates measure the store instead of matching constants
- Fixed: Keep the Windows store root in a DPAPI-protected per-user file so delete-all destroys it
- Fixed: Make the DPAPI protector crate-visible and the RSS pattern reader unix-only so Windows compiles
- Added: Refuse attribution lines at push and at merge with a pre-push hook, scripts/merge-pr.sh, a tested CI script and tracked Claude Code settings, and lint line endings, the changelog and help text read by line number
- Changed: Decide every attribution check by one predicate, write the changelog as one flat list of typed entries, pair each rule in AGENTS.md with its check and its limits, and remove comments that restate the code in the commit-message check
- Fixed: Check out shell scripts and git hooks with LF in every clone, and stop the commit-msg hook from refusing a clean git commit -v
- Fixed: Release the store's writer lock explicitly so a spawned child cannot keep it held
- Added: Add the per-run ci.d driver with the nine R1 checks, the bounds file of the R1 gate, its memory check and its mutant rows beside the code
- Changed: Give each CI job a 30-minute timeout, cancel only superseded pull_request runs, save the Rust cache on main only and list the workspace members by a glob
- Fixed: Make the R1 gate checks fail closed when a source did not run, the input counts of a run contradict its input mode, a bound is unreadable, a verify step outlives its deadline, a ci.d entry is not a regular NN-name.sh file, a mutant had no build of its kind, or the tree changed during the run
- Security: Pin every workflow action to a commit, give the workflow read-only permissions and let Dependabot propose action updates
- Added: Add the page-test harness under tools/r4-page with its pinned packages, the browser builds pinned by address, size and SHA-256 and not committed, the six accessibility checks and a self-test on a sample view
- Added: Add docs/r2 with its README and the .gitattributes files that give the workflow run's directories LF line ends and keep its fixtures out of line-end conversion
- Added: Add the learning run's first phase: the r5-stat trial of the gate statistics with the tools/r5-trials workspace, docs/r5/targets.toml and the attribute files of tools/r5-trials, docs/r5 and fixtures/r5; the r5-routine trial, which fixes the three routine constants in docs/r5/targets.toml at 3-60-5, with its seeds file, its result file and the sample of 135 trains in fixtures/r5/trains/sample.jsonl; the r5-drift drift harness and admission trial: the case reader of contracts.md L9, the five counts of the L9.3 paragraph, the page model, the arms none, plain and admit, the 16 development cases under fixtures/r5/cases/dev and docs/r5/cases.md; docs/r5/heldout.txt with the SHA-256 of the archive of the 24 held-out cases, which stay outside the repository until freeze point 2; the result of the admission trial in docs/r5/trials/admission.md, whose verdict is rule=admit, and freeze point 1 in docs/r5/freeze.toml; and docs/r5/README.md and docs/r5/plan-revisions.md, which say what each file of the run's directory holds and which lines of docs/plan.md the run revises
- Added: Add ADRs 0004 to 0012 for what the capture run decided, docs/privacy-limits.md on what expiry and delete-all cover and which private windows capture tells, and a ci.d check of the ADR headers
- Changed: Name the capture daemon in docs/plan.md as the only opener of the capture store and the app as its starter, with a dated revision line for each edit
- Fixed: Cite the research lines that state the facts in ADRs 0001 and 0003 and record the crates of the workspace in ADR 0002, each by a dated amendment
- Added: Add three units of the agent-setup run: the local fixture sites (utility, payment frame, shop, mail and calendar faces, control) with three invented seeds, nine variants, a state object and a self-test, listed in docs/r3/fixtures.md; the web executor trial (tools/r3-trials/web), a chromiumoxide adapter, nine capability steps run with no model against the fixture sites, a fault proxy that makes each step miss, and the result file docs/r3/trials/web-executor.md; and the sheet trial of U0.4 with its corpus of eight workbooks and its result, which names umya-spreadsheet for U2.3
- Added: Add the router crate halvo-route with its tiers, call kinds, categories, line, price file and policy types, and the gate tool route-gate with its generator, scorer, self-test and the check 30-r6-table; the gateway request crate halvo-route-wire, which reads and writes the body of a hosted model call and reads its two answers, with its limits, schema and vectors under docs/r6/gateway/ and the check 20-r6-gateway; LF line ends under docs/r6/ and scripts/r6/ in every clone, with docs/r6/README.md saying what its directories hold; and the record of which of the four routing models may enter the model trials, with their licence lines and the size and SHA-256 of each 4-bit file that enters
- Changed: Give each CI job a 60-minute timeout in place of 30 minutes, so that the checks of the later runs fit, with the bound ci_job_timeout_min and the self-test case that reads it
- Added: Add docs/r4/alive.md, the interface run's table of which parts of Halvo may be alive at once, with the bound in bytes of each part for which another run has set one, and docs/r4/README.md, which says what each file of docs/r4 holds and which unit adds it
- Added: Add the crate halvo-schema with the id types, the parsed app, domain and site types, the workflow, summary, support and evidence records, the request envelopes over four roles with their compile-fail test, the two strict decoders and the reply writer; and `route-gate import`, which reads the trial files docs/r3/trials/driver.md and docs/r3/trials/model-cells.md and writes the four outcome files, with three golden cases and the check 35-r6-outcomes
- Added: Add docs/r2/category-parameters.md, the table that gives each of the ten seed categories its four parameters from the capture layer (category, site, trigger and recurrence), for later rows with the source open to join
- Added: Add the browser profile trial of the agent-setup run (tools/r3-trials/profile): a session exported from one headless profile and imported into a new empty one, a search of the removed profile's parent and of the run's temporary entries for the page marker, and the tool tree-peak for the memory of a process tree; the web trial library gains the export and import of cookies and localStorage, a start that makes the profile and a temporary directory of the browser's own and refuses an existing or linked path, and a stop that removes both, ends only processes that still have their recorded creation time and reports every process id of the tree, with the results in docs/r3/trials/browser-profile.md
- Added: Add the crate halvo-coord of the interface run: the coordinator's ids, names, limits, ports and memory ports, the words table with its export, the list of the page's commands and the 35 page fixtures written from the Rust types; and scripts/r6/layout.txt, the routing run's 66 path patterns with the unit that owns each, and the check 10-r6-layout.sh, which fails when a tracked file under one of the run's seven roots matches no pattern and, called with --all, when a pattern of a unit not named after --waits has no file
- Fixed: Make scripts/prerequisites.sh create `~/.cargo/bin` before it copies the prebuilt cargo-release there, so that a machine with no such directory, such as the hosted macOS runner, gets the prebuilt binary and does not build the tool from source; when the download, the unpacking or the copy fails, the script names that step before it falls back to cargo install; with `sh scripts/prerequisites.sh --self-test`
- Added: Add crates/halvo-cap, which runs one child under a memory cap and reads its peak (a Job object on Windows; on macOS a 250 ms poll of the lifetime peak physical footprint and a kill on a breach), with the binary measure-peak and its control child, the per-user model lock, the example hold that probes what the reading does not count (the 8 names of the macOS list and the 4 of the Windows list), scripts/r2/ci.d/11-cap.sh and docs/r2/trials/cap.md; the halvo-redact crate, whose mask function replaces card numbers by the Luhn check, IBANs by mod 97, US Social Security numbers by format and keys by 72 prefixes of 27 rules of the default configuration of gitleaks v8.30.1 with a token, with its list of documented misses and the ci.d check 22-redact; and three cases on two new fixture repositories in the self-test of 10-r6-layout.sh, for its glob matcher
- Added: Add tests for the rules of halvo-cap that stood without one: the Job drops its working-set maximum on the Windows error 1314 alone and returns each other error that the test tries (1200 to 1430, 2, 5, 6, 8, 87, 1450, 1453 and 1455); measure-peak exits with status 1 when any one of its three checks misses, also after a run that the cap stopped while the other checks are ok; a relative cache variable is refused with the words `error no model lock: the variable HOME is not set to an absolute path` (LOCALAPPDATA on Windows); a median equal to the limit passes the checks, and on macOS a read equal to the limit leaves a run uncapped while a read one byte above it kills the run; and the self-test of scripts/r2/ci.d/11-cap.sh reads the exit status beside the last line of enter_repository and of measure, and rejects 15 failing fixtures. The boundary test of halvo-redact plants every one of the 72 key prefixes of the table, 27,825 cases in all
- Added: tools/r2-probes, the probes of the P0 cost trials, and the trial files layer-cost.md, input-visibility.md and private-window.md under docs/r2/trials/.
- Added: Model the coordinator's schedule and its confirmations in TLA+, with the twenty-one invariants that TLC checks, each read from a history that no guard reads, and the traces that the code will replay
- Added: Add to the crate halvo-route the ruling (`ruling=none`, with a hosted value in the crate's own unit tests alone), the pick from a policy table, a call's cell and the agent's ceiling to a tier, a hand back with its cause or no model, the default cap line (2,000 minor units a month, provisional), and the module release that compiles in the ruling, the first policy table of 44 rows and the cap line and fails closed on each file that does not parse
- Added: Add the interface run's layout check: scripts/r4/layout.txt holds the 13 roots and the 78 path patterns of I14, and scripts/r4/ci.d/10-r4-layout.sh fails in both CI jobs when a tracked file under a root matches no pattern (`layout outside=<path>`), or with `--all` when a pattern has no file, with a self-test of 57 failing fixtures.
- Added: Add docs/r3/category-parameters.md, the 22 parameter rows of the agent-setup run with the source and the binding of each, docs/r3/README.md, scripts/r3/layout.txt, the 54 patterns of the run's file table, and the check 10-r3-layout, which fails when a tracked file under one of the run's roots matches no pattern
- Added: Complete the crate halvo-schema with the kind registry (field types, retention classes, origins, the four kinds of this run, the entry checks and the record check), Coarse with its compile-time assertion, the read trait and the step traits, REPLY_MAX, the four files of later runs, export-names with the NAMES lists, the golden files with their tests, the check 20-schema and tests of three rules of its first part; the record check refuses a key of a nested object that is no name, that is of digits alone or that stands twice, and a record that nests deeper than 128 levels; Coarse is sealed, so that only src/kinds.rs implements it; and tests/locked_files.rs refuses in the seven locked files a path attribute, the name include, macro_use and a call of a macro that no locked file defines, and in the four files of later runs macro_export
- Changed: An app id of halvo-schema refuses every control character of the Unicode category Cc, which is U+0000 to U+001F, U+007F and U+0080 to U+009F, where it refused U+0000 to U+001F and U+007F alone, so that an app id with a character of U+0080 to U+009F, which main took before, is refused now
- Added: Add the crate halvo-seam, the read door between the capture store and the analysis: the capture record with its reader and writer, the seam messages with their length-prefixed framing, the client, the fixture transport over batch files, the input-set reader with the reduction and docs/r2/input-sets/app-title.toml, the exported conformance suite with controls for some of its checks and a store replaced while a cursor is open, and the check 21-seam
- Added: Add to halvo-coord the items and the confirmation of the interface run (U2.2): the eight kinds with their only sources and their budgets (`raise_step`), the item as the page gets it, composed from keys of the words table, the presenter table `presenter`, the confirmation machine `confirm_step`, the rule of the guard on the link `guard_step` with the port `Policy` and its memory port, and the drive that stores the rows of an answer before it releases it, with the test presenter_table and the 20 refusal tests of I3.6 and I4.8; scripts/r4/layout.txt names src/mem/policy.rs under U2.2
- Added: Add the router `product` and the recorded mode to `route-gate score`, `derive` and `self-test` (a recorded table is scored with the ruling's tiers, a freeze file given with `--freeze` must be of its form and count the rows of the table, and a router that panics is a failed check with exit 1), docs/r6/bar.md and docs/r6/hypothesis.md, three refusal rows of the default cap line, and the check 40-r6-policy, which pins the 44 rows of the first table and the cap line, builds a copy of halvo-route with a cap file that does not parse, and fails when a cargo configuration file is tracked or when a setting of compiler flags or of the test configuration, in a form that the check reads, stands in a tracked script, a file under .github or .githooks, a TOML file, a package.json or a build script that a manifest names
- Added: Add the interface run's trial app in tools/r4-trials/app, a workspace of its own: a Tauri 2.12.5 app with a control port on 127.0.0.1, a text-only tray item, the window arms main, a, a2 and b, the sync ways get, channel and events, a content policy that keeps its page on its own scheme, and a refusal where neither CI nor R4_GUEST is set, with readers of the window and the process tree on macOS and Windows and a self-check that compares each answer with what the system shows; Add formal/r6-spend, the routing run's model of the spend rule: the machine of one hold and the device's side in TLA+ with eleven invariants that TLC checks, two controls that must fail, the script that exports traces of the machine, and 206 committed traces for the code to replay
- Added: Add the coordinator's schedule to the crate halvo-coord: the slots of a recurring workflow, the holds, start_step with its ordered steps and its queue, tick_step and the one drive function on the ports, with the fifteen refusal tests of U2.1 and drawn sequences checked against C1 and C3 to C11; a start waits until the agent store has ended the agent's newest run (ruling XB-2), a start that failed after its run_started gets its end_run from the coordinator, each move decides from what the owner answers in it, and set_state active comes from one rule in the tick (rulings XB-9, XB-10, XB-14, XB-17, IG-6); Add formal/r3-confirm, the model of the agent run's confirm policy that TLC checks: the decision table and the state machine of one effect with their eleven invariants, a control copy that must fail, the export script, and the two files that the code will be compared with, the 3,840 rows of the decision and 400 traces of the state machine; Add the interface run's glossary docs/r4/words.md, its never-read list scripts/r4/words/never-read.txt and the words check scripts/r4/ci.d/45-r4-words.sh, whose script scripts/r4/words/lint.mjs reads every sentence of the words table, of the 18 state fixtures and of the two view headings in both CI jobs (I1.4 to I1.6)
- Changed: Rewrite the sentences of the interface run's words table in crates/halvo-coord/src/words.rs for a person with no technical background, and write the 35 page fixtures under ui/tests/fixtures/coord again from them (I1.1).
- Added: Add the account workspace of the billing run under account/: the backend wire contract vendored at one pinned commit with the sha256 of each file (account/schema/, and vendor.sh to vendor it again), the crate halvo-wire with the types that typify 0.8.0 generates from the schema and a request builder whose property test finds no key outside the schema, account/deny.toml, and scripts/r7/ci.d/10-r7-account.sh, which reads account/ through cargo metadata and builds, tests, deny-checks, format-checks and lints it offline in both CI jobs, with the cargo-deny 0.20.2 that scripts/prerequisites.sh now installs from its release after a sha256 check and the packages that it now fetches; add the egress check scripts/egress/check.sh, which runs a command and denies its process tree every TCP connect, UDP send, ICMP datagram and name lookup outside a list file, or logs it where its mode cannot stop it so that the run exits 1, with its sandbox mode for macOS, which also denies lookups through the resolver's service and requests handed to the URL session service, its wfp mode for Windows, which runs the command without administrator rights, logs and does not stop name lookups, loopback connects and connects of binaries that the tree starts later, and restores the settings that it changed, its probes, self-test and tests, the per-push file 10-egress.sh and the trial docs/r2/trials/egress.md; add the learning run's layout file scripts/r5/layout.txt with the attribute file of scripts/r5, the check 10-r5-layout.sh that holds every tracked file under the run's roots to it and requires docs/r5/freeze.toml with the hashes of the frozen files, the check 40-r5-trials.sh that runs each trial's refusals, tests and block on both jobs, requires each trial's result file and builds every trial with overflow checks on, and the ignore line for tools/r5-trials/target; add to the crate halvo-route the spend rule's machine of one hold (`spend::step`), the admission test and the cap states (`admit`), and the grant without which no part of a model request leaves the machine (`leave::grant`), which makes one only in a build that sets `cfg(test)`, as the crate's own unit tests do, and never under the release ruling, with the 19 refusal cases of T7.4, T8.4 and T9.4 and the test `no_grant_in_release`; the crate forbids unsafe code
- Fixed: The conformance suite of halvo-seam reports four more kinds of wrong server, each with a control: a filtered cursor opened at a position other than zero that serves from the start of the store, another store id at the zero position that opens a cursor, a list of kinds in another order than C4.4's that gets bad-request, and a position two past the last line of an inner segment that gets bad-position; the four checks of the suite that compare a server's replies and had no control each have one; tests and self-test fixtures now fail on eighteen one-rule changes that passed before, which touch the client's position after store-changed or store-error to an open and its watermark and position from one Batch to the next, the fixture transport's replace mark after a close or the end of a connection, the suite's look-ahead check and its count of stops in set C, the error of an expiry segment above 2^63 - 1, and nine ways out of 21-seam.sh, whose self-test now reads the real output of the crate's tests and holds the arguments of cargo, the saved output, the skip outside CI, a failing status other than 101 and a test file whose name extends another's; and the input-set reader refuses a symbolic link, also one that names a regular file
- Security: halvo-schema forbids unsafe code, and a file of a later run can no longer build a `ParseError` that holds a text or write a record without its rules: the constructor of `ParseError`, `Strict::write_fields` and the three type macros of ids.rs take a key that only the locked modules can build, and `Unchecked` is private to request.rs, each shown by a build of the crate with a later file that tries it; the textual tests of the lock read tokens through proc-macro2 1.0.107 (MIT OR Apache-2.0, a development dependency), refuse a file that they cannot read as the compiler does, walk every file under src/ outside the seven locked files, and refuse there a path attribute, the include macros, `macro_export`, and an `impl` whose type is a type of a locked module where a path, a use, a glob, a type alias, a macro whose body names the type or a macro's `ty` argument that names it reaches it, and in a locked file `path` among the tokens of an attribute or a macro and a macro called through a macro's argument; six forms of such an `impl` in a later file still pass and are closed by a later unit (the type in round brackets, a projection of a trait of the standard library, and four that a macro builds from its arguments).
- Added: tests of halvo-schema for the changes of its rules that passed every test: a record with more than 128 lists and objects side by side is taken, a pair that stands twice is reported, the seal of `Coarse` holds against its module and `Option`, the debug form counts bytes, every value of a nested object is walked, a site value is read whole only under a key of the type Site, a nested key of 32 digits is refused, and each predicate of the textual tests has a case of its own; add the agent-setup run's file of halvo-schema, src/executable.rs: the agent configuration, whose allow list holds only send, delete and submit; the executable step and the playbook; the ten action classes, the effect states, the Cause list with ended and changed and the other closed lists; the bound object of each class; the frames between a runner and its supervisor with the confirmation and the handback; the runner bodies and the coordinator bodies with end_run, with their replies; the record shapes of the family agent and the kinds agent_config and agent_run; OwnText and the run, effect and approval ids; each with a strict parser that refuses what the contract refuses and never returns a value of its input, the golden files under tests/golden/r3 and the names of these records in the names file; add the model of admission and rollback of the learning run in formal/r5-admit, which TLC checks for the fourteen properties of a learned edit, with the table of the 57 rows of an edit's states and moves, 200 traces for the code to replay, the script export.sh that writes both, and the pin of the TLC release; add the model cells of the agent-setup run (U0.5): tools/r3-trials/model/ with weights.txt (the publishers' own 4-bit GGUF files of six models, each by its SHA-256 at a pinned commit), fetch.txt and fetch.sh, the trial loader r3-model (llama.cpp on the CPU with the weights read into memory, under the model lock of halvo-cap), run.sh and fits.py, and docs/r3/trials/model-cells.md with the footprint cells, the bounds and the verdict line.
- Fixed: Close the gaps of the words check 45-r4-words (U1.3b): a spaced string after a template literal in a view, a word break at U+0085, the setup exits for a script that cannot enter its own directory or find its repository, other-view strings with no space, and a closed name of three parts written with hyphens each have a failing fixture; a glossary without one of the four rows that I1.6 fixes, or with a table line after the table's end, fails with glossary; a name of the code's closed lists written with an underscore or a hyphen, and Sonnet, Opus, Haiku or Fable with a capital inside a sentence, fail as never_read; and a self-test started inside the self-test ends at once.
- Changed: The class address of the words check passes a site's name, its host name with its port where that is not the usual one (pay.site1.example:8443), and still flags a scheme and a path, a query or a fragment after a host (I1.4, ruling IG-14).
- Added: Add the crate halvo-sqlcipher, which builds SQLCipher from the pinned source for every crate that opens an encrypted database, the store's page reader and its tests of the raw pages, the segment format, the key wrap, the wrap nonce, the wipe on drop and each rule of the store and deletion checks, and the store's mutant rows beside its code; add the break proofs of the formal checks as mutant rows beside the store and custody code, and docs/verify/check-covered.sh with a ci.d check that every line of the covered table in docs/verify/store-shred.md names a required row whose verdict is a formal check; add to the first formal check of the store the moves that fail or are refused (a create that fails after its database, a create under a root that exists, a delete-all that stops after its root step and is called again, a second destroy of the root, an append whose index row fails), in a second random test and in a test with a fixed order of moves, with a mutant row for each of their reads that a mutant of the store can break; add to halvo-coord the setup flow of the item setup (U2.2b): setup_step, in which the answer that gives the last parameter makes the one body set_bindings with the chosen or typed values, and answer_setup and tick_setup, which send it, owe it until the owner acknowledges it, refuse an answer with state while it is owed, close the item at the acknowledgement and then run the rule of active once; the line confirm.changed above an effect whose newest decision released in the run and this life is allow_once, read through Table::newest_allows from the release numbers of the guard's table; and the tests refusal_setup_owed, refusal_mixed_request and refusal_changed_line; tests of the gate tool that read the keys `rows=`, `models=`, `date=`, `driver=` and `writer=` of a freeze file by their names, a repeated origin and six origins in all in the test `ruling_test_value` of halvo-route, and four failing fixtures of the self-test of scripts/r6/ci.d/40-r6-policy.sh: the cause `derive` named over `score`, the cause `score` named over `bar`, a composite action under .github and an interpreter line with a space after `#!`, each of which the check reads today; the test of `route-gate score` on a table with the header alone now checks every line that it prints.
- Changed: Return a typed error for each state of the encrypted store and of key custody: a missing, corrupt or refused root, a root state that can clear, a custody kind that the build lacks, a wrap that does not open, an unparseable store-key file, a live segment without its file, an I/O fault with its kind and a database fault with its SQLite code; `--verify-deletion` prints `root_destroyed=yes` only when the root is missing and the retained key does not open under an all-zero root, and on any other unwrap error prints no line and exits 1; `--verify-store` exits 1 when a file of its store holds the raw store key; make the first formal check of the store decide that a shredded key is gone from the decrypted pages, WAL frames and journal records of the store files, require that the root seals the store key and is destroyed by delete-all, and make the second check take only a classified refusal of a second writer and require one in each run; name in docs/verify/store-shred.md the custody functions that seal the store key and fill a new root since create wraps under a new root, and say what a second delete-all on a handle returns; make the hold setup read the one table of parameters in src/items, remove origins from the body set_bindings, which holds slots alone and which the memory owner merges by name and answers with revision 1 for an agent's first body, and give agents_answer and agents_setup_typed the cause state, with commands.json written again.
- Fixed: Make a failed store create remove the database files it made (a directory that it cannot write keeps them, and a later create over them starts from nothing), never destroy in its clean-up the root of another store, also one that it cannot read or that a create at the same instant made, and never take the root of a store that was created before it (a create under the same root id whose check passed before the other's root existed can still replace that root: two creates under one root id at the same instant stay under Not covered in docs/verify/store-shred.md), let a delete-all that failed after destroying its root finish on the next call, fail a shred whose write-ahead log cannot be read, report a locked keychain and a DPAPI state that can clear as RootLocked, accept no custody kind for release whose delete-all cannot finish, keep the store and segment keys on the heap so that a move copies no key byte, append an event's two rows together or not at all, zero and remove a partial root file with its root, open a store-key file whose wrapped key has another length than a wrap as unparseable, and find the store key at rest also as unpadded hex, inside a longer base64 text and as a list of decimal numbers; title a confirmation whose effects have more than one class with confirm.mixed.title and its two controls with confirm.mixed.reject and confirm.mixed.allow, which name no class, and put before the fields of each effect a line confirm.<class>.action that names its own action, so that a person never allows a deletion under a control that says send; `route-gate score` and `route-gate derive` refuse a freeze file whose line `on_device` names the model `synthetic` as its driver or its writer, with `REFUSED file: --freeze line 2 is not of the freeze form`, because a token of that line is a model token other than `synthetic` (T5.2).
- Security: The test value of the router's ruling is made by the crate halvo-route's own code alone: its payload `ruling::TestOrigins` has a private field and no public maker, and `Ruling::test_hosted` is private to the crate, so a program of another crate built with `cfg(test)` set from outside (by `RUSTFLAGS`, `CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS` or a cargo configuration file) stops compiling where it names the test value, and `leave::grant` answers `ruling` there as in the product; the crate now builds under such a flag, because only its integration test tests/no_grant_in_release.rs names its development dependency; src/lib.rs, src/leave.rs, src/ruling.rs and src/tier.rs are pinned whole, their test modules included, by their SHA-256 in the one table of the test `pinned_files_hold_their_hashes` in tests/no_grant_in_release.rs, so a change of any of them comes with a new hash line in the same commit, which the pin of leave.rs above its test module alone did not ensure; two routes to the test value stay open, each a change of several files that writes a maker outside src/ruling.rs and src/leave.rs, and are written down for the next unit of the routing run.
- Added: Add the check scripts/r4/ci.d/30-r4-formal.sh, which runs the small configurations of the coordinator's two models with TLC in both CI jobs, compares the committed traces with a fresh export and with the stamp of the model files, and proves with two control copies that it can fail; the reply form of halvo-schema carries an error name that a file of a later run declares (U1.1d, rulings S-35, S-43 and S-45): each of the four later files holds a list `ERROR_NAMES`, one item at the top level of the file whose elements are string literals, which `kinds::declared_error_names()` chains as four `&'static [&'static str]`, and a test holds the names that the crate reads to the literals that the lexer reads from the four files; `ErrorName::Declared` holds such a name as the string of the list that declares it, and neither a later file nor another crate can build one by hand; `ErrorName::read` takes the nine names and the declared ones, and `decode_reply` and `write_reply` take and write a declared name, while a name that no file declares is still `malformed` and no `ErrorName` can hold it.
- Changed: Change the coordinator's models: a repeated `approved` record passes and marks nothing, a `releasing` record with no approval passes only where the policy table gives `run`, a second request for an effect comes only after an `allow_once` for it, and the schedule holds the agent store's view of a run, `end_run`, the answers of `reap` and a twelfth invariant, C12
- Fixed: Fix formal/r4-coordinator/export.sh, which refused with the cause `java` when a line stood before the version line of `java -version`
- Added: Add the run to the crate halvo-coord (interface run, U2.3): run_step with the moves of I5.6, the stop with Runs::cancel and Table::stop and its kill at the first tick after cancel_grace_s, the end of a stopping run whose kill the library refused with unknown_run, the recovery of a new life (Runs::reap first, every ledger event of each agent, run_ended with interrupted, the owed end_run of a run that the store's record does not name, item_lapsed with app_ended), a workflow that is gone (delete_step, forgotten), the item check raised where the store's record first shows an unknown effect, the test formal_traces that replays both trace files of the models, and the refusal tests of outlives_app, stop_tree, deleted_workflow, interrupted_no_retry, reap_alive, check_raised and gone_outlives_app. The crate halvo-learn holds the rule of admission and rollback of a learned edit and the routine step as pure functions with no dependency (learning run, U1.2): `edit_step` follows the 57 rows of `formal/r5-admit/edit-table.tsv`, which the test `edit_table` compares row by row, `routine_step` gives the events of `fixtures/r5/trains/sample.jsonl` in the test `routine_sample`, and eleven refusal tests hold admission, the run rules and the source rule; the check `20-r5-rule.sh` counts the 29 tests of the rule's closed list in every push and fails when one that its crate holds does not read ok; 16 of the 29 come with later units and count as pending until then. The self-test of scripts/r6/ci.d/40-r6-policy.sh (routing run, U1.2c) has the fixture `github-other-file`, a tracked file under `.github/` that is neither a workflow nor an action file and sets the flags variable, whose directory and file name, the name with no extension, the self-test draws anew on each run, so a copy of the check whose scan reads under `.github/` only `workflows/` and `actions/*/action.yml`, any other fixed list of directories, or only names that end in `.yml`, `.yaml` or `.env`, fails its self-test. Add the crate halvo-secrets (billing run, 7.2) under account/crates/, where the account helper keeps its credential: a file of mode 0600 in a directory of mode 0700 on macOS (a file or directory of a wider mode, of another owner, with an access control list or with a second name is refused and kept), the Keychain under the feature keychain that a Developer ID release sets, and a file sealed by DPAPI in the scope of the user on Windows. A read answers not stored, found, locked or a fault with its code, and a read that fails is never not stored; a write only creates; a delete removes the one item that the store takes; a read is not stored only when the store directory or the item is absent under a parent that exists, a missing step above it is a fault, and a write makes the store directory alone; on macOS the store walks the caller's path as given, follows at most 32 links, each read relative to the directory that holds it, refuses a directory on the way that someone other than the person and root may change or a link of another owner, and makes every call through the one directory handle that it checked; on Windows it makes its directory with a protected access list for the person, SYSTEM and Administrators, refuses a directory of another owner, and reads only "file not found" as no item, also for a delete; the secret is never printed and is overwritten with zeros when it is dropped. The check 10-r7-account lints account/ with all its features and, on a GitHub-hosted runner only, runs the hosted test of each store, which prints store=keychain and store=file in the macos job and store=dpapi in the windows job. Add the stand-in for the account backend (billing run, 7.5a) under account/stub/, a Node package with no dependency that reads only the vendored schema, route table and samples: it judges each request in the order of contract 1.4, answers from the samples in the scripted states of a checkout, an account and a delete with test calls on a second loopback port, records every request byte for byte, serves over TLS with a test authority that it makes in memory, and runs its self-test in both CI jobs as 20-r7-stub.
- Changed: In halvo-coord (interface run, U2.3), Runs::reap answers one gone or alive per mark of the supervisor library, and the memory Runs answers started, unknown_run and ended and records every call as made in tried; the schedule's stop_run is public, a start that stored run_started reads its agent's newest run as not ended by the store until the owner is read again (ruling XB-24), and an owed end_run or run_ended of a workflow that is gone is sent once more and ended by the refusal no_workflow (ruling XB-26).
- Fixed: In halvo-coord (interface run, U2.3), an owed run_ended that the owner stored with a lost acknowledgement is paid once the owner's events, read again after the refusal seq, hold it, where it held its agent for the rest of the life; the item check and recovery's body that the owner refuses with seq are logged once more after the owner's events read again, and the agent holds an item of the coordinator that those events left open, where a lost acknowledgement kept the check from being raised for the rest of the life; the item_lapsed entries of a recovery body that the owner does not acknowledge are owed with its run_ended and a later tick stores them, where they were dropped and the item stayed open; a tick whose clock reads earlier than a run's stop kills that run and the tree of a run that ended after its stop, where a clock set back after a stop delayed the kill by the size of the step; an owed run_ended or item_lapsed that the agent's events already hold is paid with no log, where a read after a refusal seq let one run end twice or one item lapse twice; and a debt's read of the owner's events after a refusal seq takes up an open item of the coordinator as the check's read does, where a check whose acknowledgement was lost was raised a second time; the person's answer to the hold check closes only an open item of the kind check and leaves a ready item open; and the memory owner refuses an end_run of a removed workflow's run with no_workflow, as the real owner does. The check 10-r7-account (billing run, 7.2) formats every crate of account/ with `cargo fmt --all --check`; without --all, cargo fmt given the manifest of account/ found no target once account/ held a second crate.
- Security: The product's ruling is `none`: this build has no hosted tier, and the router's test value exists only where `cfg(test)` is set. No file of the crate halvo-route but src/ruling.rs can name the maker `Ruling::test_hosted` or the payload's type `TestOrigins`, or set its origins, whatever flags set `cfg(test)` (`RUSTFLAGS`, `CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS` or a cargo configuration file): the maker is private to src/ruling.rs and the type stands in a module private to that file, so a maker written in another file, also in a test module, stops the build with E0624, which closes the two routes that the earlier Security entry on the router's test value left open; other files can name the variant `Ruling::TestHosted` and, from the value that `ruling::admit_under_test_value` hands to the body of `admit`, hold or make again a test value with no origin, under which `leave::grant` makes no grant. src/ruling.rs holds the one `Drop` of `Ruling` in a test build, so no other file can write a `Drop` that keeps a test value as it is dropped (E0119); the tests of src/leave.rs get a test value through `Ruling::test_hosted_for_leave` with a key, `leave::TestKey`, that src/leave.rs alone can make; the test `release_policy_fails_closed` of src/release.rs no longer reads the policy under the test value. crates/halvo-route/Cargo.toml is pinned whole as the fifth entry of `PINNED`. A change of a pinned file shows as a changed hash line in `PINNED` in the same commit, for the reviewer of that commit, and the test `pinned_files_hold_their_hashes`, which compares the pins, runs only while cargo builds tests/no_grant_in_release.rs and the crate is a member of the workspace; that holds for that earlier entry's sentence on the four source files too. So a `[lib]` table with a path that points the crate's root at another file, a build key, a lints table or any other change of the manifest fails that test, while cargo builds and runs it, unless the same commit updates the manifest's hash, and a manifest that stops that test from being built switches the pins off. Two kinds of change to the repository's own build stay open and are written down for the routing run's checks: a change that stops that test from being built or run, or that leaves it running while it compares less, and code that runs at build time and rewrites a source file.
- Added: Add the permanent functional check of the Windows capture sources (capture run, win-1): crates/halvo-capture/tests/windows_functional.rs, whose tests each build the sources they need in their own process on the windows-2025 runner, and scripts/r1/ci.d/50-windows-functional.sh, which the windows job runs: it builds the test binary once with --locked, fails when the tests that the binary lists differ from the script's own list, runs each test in its own process under a time limit, copies each test's output into the log under a `    | ` prefix, prints one `WF <test> result=...` line for each test and the line `WF total=<n> pass=<n> known_defect=<n> fail=<n>`, writes the same lines to target/ci-artifacts/50-windows-functional/wf.txt, and fails when a test fails, crashes or reaches its limit; each of the 11 known defects is a test in `mod known_defect` that must fail as one failed test with exactly one `KNOWN-DEFECT <id>` line, with the id that the script's list gives it and no panic of another thread, and a listed defect that passes fails the job with `result=FIXED-BUT-LISTED`, so the unit that fixes a defect moves its test out of that module and its row out of the script's list in the same pull request; the self-test holds one failing fixture for each refusal of the script and fails when it rejects fewer. Add the crate halvo-sheet, the sheet executor (agent-setup run, U2.3): read of a range through calamine 0.38.0, and read_source of a CSV file by a parser of the crate's own; prepare, which refuses before any write a workbook over the bounds of size, with a pivot table, a macro or a data validation, with a relationship that does not name a part, with a part it cannot patch, or with a cell to write in a table header or in an array or shared formula, and otherwise writes a copy of its own call beside the run's checkpoint, the package copied entry by entry with one worksheet part patched at the written cells and fullCalcOnLoad set, and requires that every other byte of the copy is the source's and that the written cells read back; and replace, which puts the copy in the workbook's place in one rename or leaves the workbook as it was; a link, a directory or a path outside the given folder is refused. Add the crate halvo-policy, the confirm policy as pure functions (agent-setup run, U1.2): the class of a step and the check of a playbook, the decision of the table and the typed decision that only `decide` makes, the step of an effect's state with its count of rebinds and its limit, the digest of a bound object, the admission of its fields, the presets of the ten seed categories with their validation, the limits, and the command agent-config (A1.3, A2.4, A2.5, A3.3, A4.3, A5.1 to A5.5, A9.3).
- Changed: The locked types of halvo-schema stand behind a crate boundary (workflow run, U1.1e, ruling S-47): the new crate halvo-schema-core holds src/ids.rs, category.rs, site.rs, record.rs, request.rs and kinds.rs, a lib.rs that forbids unsafe code, the four list files executable.rs, coordinator.rs, learning.rs and routing.rs that hold each later run's `KINDS` and `ERROR_NAMES`, NOTICE and the pin of psl; halvo-schema depends on it, re-exports its modules and each run's two lists at their old paths, and keeps the later files, export-names and its tests, so every path that later code writes resolves as before; the lock test holds both lib.rs files to their form, the core crate's set of files, both manifests, no build script, the path attribute and the include macros out of both crates' sources, and each list file to a closed grammar whose values it holds to the names that a build reads; the test of later-file builds builds a fixture of two crates and shows that a foreign trait, an inherent impl or a blanket impl for a core type does not compile in a later file, that From and PartialEq with a later type and a later trait reached by a glob import do, and that no core file can name halvo-schema; the lock test also holds each later file's re-export of its two lists, once at the top level with no attribute and no other item of either name; scripts/r2/ci.d/20-schema.sh runs the tests of both packages and requires, under each test target, the ok line of every test that scripts/r2/20-schema-tests.txt lists, so a manifest key, an ignore or a test table that keeps a listed test from running fails it.
- Removed: The lock test of halvo-schema (workflow run, U1.1e) no longer reads impls in a later file and no longer refuses `macro_export` or `macro_use` there, nor holds the form of the locked files by tokens (rulings S-47 (4) and S-49): across the crate boundary the compiler refuses an inherent impl, a foreign trait and a blanket impl for a core type, and no line of the core crate can name later code.
- Added: The account backend of R7 (billing run, F.1) lives under backend/, brought as files from da7d398, the main of the repository where it was built, without that repository's history, with its model under formal/r7-ledger/. Five checks run it in the one workflow through the driver, as scripts/r7/ci.d/10-r7-layout (with scripts/r7/layout.txt), 20-r7-backend-static, 21-r7-backend-test (with scripts/r7/backend-tests.txt, the test files and their counts), 22-r7-backend-records and 23-r7-machine-paths, and docs/r7/gate/ holds a stamped record of each line of the backend's gate for the tree of backend/ that it ran on, which scripts/r7/stamp-gate-records.sh writes. In halvo-coord (interface run, U2.2c), the second step of a sign_in item: the run's event Bind raises an item that shows the site's name of the origin with the choices decline and allow, stores its item_answered row with the whole origin before Runs::answer_bind gets the row's answer, drops a refusal ended, and at a lapse answers decline before it stops the run; the setup drive's three groups of refusal names, where a body refused by config, malformed, too_large or length makes the same item ask every parameter again with the line item.setup.not_taken; the fact begun of an effect in the guard's table (Table::stored) and raise_confirm, by which a confirm request for an effect that has begun or whose newest released decision is reject_once ends the run with kind and shows no card, while nothing in the crate calls either yet: the link of U5.4 is to call Table::stored at each acknowledgement of an effect record, and U2.4's coordinator raise_confirm for each confirm request; the words entries item.sign_in.bind, choice.sign_in.bind_decline, choice.sign_in.bind_allow, item.link_account.origin and item.setup.not_taken, and the fixture state needs_sign_in_bind.
- Changed: ADR 0002 (billing run, F.1) records the owner's word of 2026-11-12 that the backend lives in this repository; scripts/prerequisites.sh installs pnpm from the archive whose sha512 backend/package.json pins, the backend's packages and its pinned tools; the backend's gate checks and backend/scripts/check-machine-paths.sh take backend/ inside the work tree; check 95 and the model's scripts read the model at formal/r7-ledger/; and the two ci.d self-test steps of the workflow give git the CI job's name and email. In halvo-coord (interface run, U2.2c), Origin::parse takes an origin as the agent-setup run's WebOrigin::parse takes it (http or https, labels of 1 to 63 bytes with no hyphen at either end, a host of at most 253 bytes, a port from 1 to 65,535); confirm.new_site and the link_account item show a site by its host with an unusual port and no scheme, and the item_answered row of a link_account answer holds the whole origin; answer_setup and tick_setup take the agent's ledger and the limits, answer_handback takes the handback request, and compose_setup takes whether the item asks again; the words check 45-r4-words stops with input where its list of states and the state files of ui/tests/fixtures/coord differ in either direction. On a push to main, scripts/r1/ci.d/30-mutants.sh (capture run, mutants-1) no longer runs every mutant: it runs rows in full for at most its budget of seconds (budget_s, 240 s to begin with, counted from the first row's start; it starts no row after it and lets a running row end), in the order of the SHA-256 of the commit id and the row's name, so a second run of a commit takes the same rows and the next commit takes others; it prints one line for each row that ran with its seconds and a line `ran <n> of <m> rows`, and fails when a row that ran is not caught, when the runner prints no count line, when no row ran or when the budget is not a number or is below 1 second; scripts/r1-gate/mutation-test.sh runs `--only` rows in the order given and takes `--budget SECONDS` for this; the whole list stays a line of the gates that run on a person's machine.
- Fixed: In halvo-coord (interface run, U2.2c), a setup whose set_bindings the owner refused by a name after which another value can pass no longer leaves a card that refuses every answer while the body is sent again at each tick, and the refusal binding ends the setup's debt; one item_answered row is stored for each setup item when it closes. scripts/r1/ci.d/30-mutants.sh (capture run, mutants-1) removes the temporary directories of its runs and of its self-test by its own trap on every way out, and its runs of scripts/r1-gate/mutation-test.sh take --remove-scratch, so a run of the step no longer leaves a copy of the tree and its target directory under TMPDIR.
- Added: The schema file of the learning run (learning run, U1.3, contracts.md L1 and L2.2): `halvo_schema::learning` reads and writes the records `learn_run`, `learn_edit` with its `LearnedTarget`, and `learn_routine`, and the coordinator body `list_refinements` (`LearnedRead`, a `CoordinatorBody` whose reply is `Refinements`), each with a parser that takes a JSON object only and refuses every form that the contract refuses with a `Refusal` that holds no value; the core list file registers `learn_run` and `learn_routine`, bounded at 93 days and of origin capture with the workflow id under the key `workflow`, and declares the error name `binding`; tests/golden/r5/ holds the three examples and the request with its reply, and export-names writes the run's names into tests/golden/names.txt. The agent-setup run's schema file holds the body `add_origin` with its three ways `sign_in`, `account_link` and `new_origin` and the revision reply, the frames `bind`, `bind_reply`, `browser` (with the browser's process id, its creation time in microseconds and its process group) and `browser_reply`, the Cause `secret` for a run whose token store could not be read, and `WebOrigin::of_address`, which makes the origin of an address that a browser reports and refuses every address that could name another site (agent-setup run, U1.1b, contracts.md A6.2, A7.2 and A7.4, rulings CP-80 and CP-81).
- Changed: The body `set_bindings` of halvo-schema holds slots alone and refuses the key `origins`, and `secret_reply` holds one of three answers, `stored` with its value, `none`, or `unreadable` with the member `locked` or `fault`, so that a store that could not be read never reads as no secret, and `CoordinatorReply::answers` takes the reply `{"rev":1}` alone as the answer to `create_agent`, as contracts.md A7.2 gives it, where it took any revision (agent-setup run, U1.1b).
- Fixed: formal/r5-admit/export.sh (learning run, U1.1b) makes its temporary directories under TMPDIR and removes them by a trap on every path out; on macOS its bare `mktemp -d` ignored TMPDIR and left one directory in the user's temporary directory at every run, also at every run of the check 30-r5-formal and of each fixture of its self-test that runs the committed export.sh.
- Added: The check 30-r5-formal of the model of admission and rollback (formal/r5-admit; learning run, U1.1b): every push runs TLC with the 14 properties at the small bound of Small.cfg, exports the table and the traces again and compares them byte for byte with the committed files, runs two control copies that must break Q1 and Q5, and fails with `record` unless formal/r5-admit/record.txt, the committed record of the full bound, the small run's count of states and every run of formal/r5-admit/runs.txt, carries the stamp of the model's files; Admit.cfg and Small.cfg are accepted in one fixed form, every file that the check hashes or compares must be text with eol=lf, a failure writes fix.txt into the artifacts directory, and formal/r5-admit/record.sh writes the record, one TLC run for each call; the first record is committed with it.
- Added: The capture sink (capture run, core-1) numbers each event at its one receiver (`SinkWriter::recv_timeout` and `try_recv` return a `Received` with an `ArrivalNumber`), draws the pause barrier there (`SinkWriter::barrier`), counts accepted, dropped and refused emits for each kind and holds a coalesced count for core-2 to fill (`SinkHandle::counts`) and takes a non-zero capacity (`sink::bounded`); the source trait gains `begin`, `shutdown` and `health` with default bodies beside `start`, `stop` and `modality`; `CaptureError` compares and clones and gains `Timeout`, `Os`, `ThreadEnded` and `AlreadyStarted`; six sink mutants are standing rows in crates/halvo-capture/src/mutants.rows. (capture run, control-1) Add the crate halvo-control, which the app and the capture daemon share and which links no store code: the stored model types, the onboarding types, the data directory with the registry of every entry Halvo keeps under it, the control endpoint keyed by the data directory, the service arguments, the one builder and writer of the login item's plist and the Windows start value; and the check 19-control-links-no-store, which fails when halvo-control's tree holds halvo-capture, halvo-sqlcipher, rusqlite or libsqlite3-sys.
- Removed: In halvo-capture (capture run, core-1), `SinkWriter::receiver`, which nothing called and which handed out events with no arrival number. (capture run, control-1) Remove crates/halvo-capture/src/service/, the capture agent's plist writer and the Windows scheduled-task installer, which nothing called.
- Fixed: In halvo-capture (capture run, core-1), the sink tests fail, and do not hang, on a sink that drops keystrokes on a full queue, blocks on a frame or answers `Queued` for a keystroke that a gone writer refused; sink.rs no longer claims one appender to the store, and source.rs no longer calls the emit path non-blocking; a capture source that implements neither `begin` and `shutdown` nor `start` and `stop` panics with fixed text at its first call on every ordinary path, though not in a thread-local destructor that runs after the guard's list is gone. (capture run, control-1) Refuse a stored storage cap below 1 GiB or above 1 TiB instead of raising it to 1 GiB, refuse a day that does not exist or lies outside 0000 to 9999 without a panic, give each capture kind and capture state one snake_case spelling, refuse an inventory with two rows for one day and kind, leave no directory behind from the consent tests, refuse a scratch form of the login item that lies in the account's LaunchAgents directory, never release a null options dictionary in the Accessibility request, refuse a scratch directory that does not resolve and judge its containment on the directory that it resolves to, on Windows by volume serial number and file index, compare the capture agent's label and the AppTranslocation component without regard to ASCII case, take away the temporary plist that the crashed install of a process no longer alive left, keep one Windows pipe name for a data directory before and after it is made, replace a Windows start value of another type, run the measuring half of check 19 in its self-test with a stand-in cargo, sweep only regular files and take the login item away even when that sweep fails, refuse a scratch form whose path as given lies in LaunchAgents, read each onboarding spelling from a JSON string alone, and free the known-folder buffer of the Windows account lookup when the lookup fails.
- Changed: In halvo-capture (capture run, core-1), two public items of the capture sink are not there unchanged, while every caller compiles: `SinkWriter::receiver` is gone, because nothing called it, and `EventSink` has a sealed supertrait, so that no other crate can implement the sink; the build also fails if `SinkWriter` ever becomes `Clone` or `Sync`. (capture run, control-1) Take one version for halvo, halvo-capture, halvo-control and halvo-sqlcipher from the root manifest, exclude account/ from the workspace, declare the empty feature r2 in halvo-capture and halvo-control, and rewrite the permission walk-back: one restart step for the screen and Input Monitoring instead of an instruction to quit, a request step that names the alert's own button and only for a permission not yet asked about, a neutral text for a lost permission, and pane titles from a table that starts at macOS 14; halvo-control denies an unsafe block without a SAFETY note.
- Added: The metering sync of the account object (billing run, 5.1) seals each sync interval once its holds have ended, sends it through the provider port once under one identifier, reads the provider's total after the uniqueness window before it sends again, closes an interval as not billable at either bound and logs one line, and cuts the open interval at a billing-period end, at a cancel, whose time comes from ended_at or the earliest stated end, and at the destroy step; an attempt whose answer a stop lost counts as unanswered, the earlier of two stated ends is kept, a reserve keeps answering after a cut that ends the open interval at its start, and a sync margin that is not above the provider call's timeout is refused. The mapping of the port's usage calls onto the provider's meter (billing run, 5.2), bound behind the port: sendUsage sends a meter event of PROVIDER_METER_EVENT and readUsageTotal reads the event summaries of the meter PROVIDER_METER_ID over whole minutes; it maps the five refusal codes of the vendored stripe package's meter error-report list that have a UsageRefusal and leaves the other five failed, sends every amount from 1 to 2^53 - 1 as plain digits, ends every meter call within PROVIDER_CALL_TIMEOUT_MS, follows no redirect, reads at most 1 MiB of an answer, needs all four provider values for each call, takes a read as a total only for one summary of exactly the minutes asked or for none, and answers failed with no connection for an id, an identifier or an event name that the meter cannot take. A case of crates/halvo-schema/tests/later_file_builds.rs (workflow run, U1.1f, the review's D1 and F2) compiles a later file that declares a role of its own and implements `ExtBody<LaterRole>` for the core record `Episode` with the reply type `Summary`, as C3.7 allows; no method of the impl runs, because `ExtBody` has none.
- Changed: The ledger (billing run, 5.1) throws a RangeError on both engines for a sync interval's sum past 2^53 - 1 and refuses a settle that would take its interval's reported sum there (ruling B-17). At its expiry (billing run, 4.5) a due hold without the dispatch mark ends voided and counts nothing, a marked one ends expired at its full amount, and a settle or a void of a hold voided at its expiry answers already-ended; the model of the cap ledger under formal/r7-ledger/ says the same, with new controls, counts, bad fixtures of check 95 and the replay of voidAtExpiry, (a) measures an admission from the state after the expiry of due holds, and (b) holds the stored expiry to the receipt's (ruling BM-11); check 50 judges both kinds of hold, flags a hold whose request reached the upstream without the dispatch mark, accepts a 409 stopped only for a hold voided at its expiry whose answer came at or after the expiry (ruling B-34), and counts on its line the rollover attempts that lacked the crossing (ruling B-15). Three tests of 5.1 give their holds the dispatch mark, and a case beside each pins that a hold without it ends voided, counts nothing and is sent to no meter (land-22, ruling P7-50); scripts/r7/backend-tests.txt lists the 58 test files of the joined tree with their counts, scripts/r7/layout.txt names the new paths of 5.1 and 5.2 under phase P5, and the 13 records of docs/r7/gate/ are stamped for the joined tree. 10-r6-layout takes a unit with one letter after its number, as the layout line of scripts/r6/required-tests.txt names U1.2d, and the last paragraph of AGENTS.md says that a unit edits that file only for a row that the coordinator rules.
- Fixed: The account object (billing run, 4.5) returns a step's committed answer when the host's alarm call throws after the commit and logs the failure as alarm-error, so that a committed go is never turned into an error (ruling B-48); a clock call or a seed whose time or anchor is above 8,640,000,000,000,000 ms, the largest time that a Date holds, is refused with 400 rejected (contract 5). scripts/r2/ci.d/20-schema.sh (workflow run, U1.1f, planner-13's ruling under S-51 (d) and ruling S-53) reads scripts/r2/20-schema-tests.txt from HEAD before cargo runs; after the run of each package's tests it takes the binary of each target that the list names from cargo's own messages (`cargo test --locked -p <package> --no-run --message-format=json`, the one test target whose source path is that file, in a call that compiles nothing of the workspace, because any artifact of that call that is not fresh fails the check with a line that names it), lists that binary's tests and its ignored tests itself, and fails when a required name is not in the first list or stands in the second; the ok line of each required test under its target stays required, and the check fails when a tracked file differs from HEAD after the tests. So it fails when a required test is renamed, removed, ignored, filtered out or left unbuilt, when its target is pointed at another file, and when a test prints the lines that the check reads. It does not hold against code of the job that writes a test binary or a tool on the PATH before the check reads them, a test that keeps its name with another body, a required target with its test harness switched off, code that runs before main in a required test file, or a cargo configuration with a runner or a compiler wrapper (ruling S-53). The script also removes its temporary directory on every way out and prints its help with `--help`.
- Security: The check 30-r6-table guards the pins of the router halvo-route from outside the crate: it requires the ok line of each test of the new list scripts/r6/required-tests.txt (the tests no_grant_in_release and pinned_files_hold_their_hashes and the 19 refusal cases) under its own target, the name in its test binary's own list and not among its ignored tests, the ok line again from a run of the required tests alone, and that each of the five pinned files hashes by the system's own tool (sha256sum, or shasum -a 256) to its entry in PINNED of tests/no_grant_in_release.rs, written as one plain declaration; the check reads the list, PINNED and the pinned files from the commit before its first cargo command and fails when a cargo command leaves a tracked file, the index or HEAD changed or adds a file to the repository, so that an ignore, a test table, `autotests = false`, a pin test weakened beside a changed pinned file or a test that rewrites what the check reads, made by a change of the crate's own files, fails it, while it does not hold code that writes outside the repository's files (a test binary under target/, cargo's configuration under CARGO_HOME or above the checkout), code that runs before main in a required test file, a target with its harness off, a line taken out of the list together with its test, or the job's environment (a runner for test binaries, a hash tool or a cargo earlier on the PATH); the rule table of AGENTS.md gains the router's row.
- Added: The six record kinds of origin own of the agent-setup run in the registry of halvo-schema-core (agent-setup run, U3.3, contracts.md A7.3 and A7.4): `agent_playbook` and `agent_bindings`, kept, `agent_effect`, `agent_log` and `agent_checkpoint`, bounded at 93 days, and `agent_session`, bounded at 35 days, each with the workflow key `workflow`, the key by which the delete cascade of the workflow run (its W-C3.10) will find a row (no cascade is on main yet), with their example rows under `crates/halvo-schema/tests/golden/r3/own/`. The crate halvo-agent-store (agent-setup run, U3.1, contracts.md A5 and A7.5): the handlers of the runner and coordinator bodies of A7.1 and A7.2 on a store held in memory, which check each effect's move with `effect_step` and its first row against the policy table for the agent's stored configuration, answer a repeated request as the first while its row is the effect's last, refuse every runner body that names a run after its end but for the repeat of its finish and of an effect's last row, move a run's `releasing` effects to `unknown` and its open ones to `rejected` at `finish` and `end_run`, and refuse a slot `workbook` that is no absolute path in one spelling. The handlers of `checkpoint`, `list_checkpoints` and `mark_checkpoint` come in a second pull request. The kind entry of `learn_edit` in halvo-schema-core (learning run, U2.3, contracts.md L1.1 and L1.3): the registry holds the learned target of a step as a kept kind of origin own in the family `learn`, with the workflow id under the key `workflow`, by which the workflow run's derived store will keep its rows until the user deletes the workflow or everything and remove them with the workflow (no store on main holds such rows yet); its fields are the row of L1.3 in order, with the values of `target` of the type `own_text`, and its example is the `learn_edit` line of `tests/golden/r5/records.jsonl`; a test of `crates/halvo-schema/tests/r5_kinds.rs` holds its class, origin, family, workflow key and fields as a closed table.
- Fixed: The router's row of AGENTS.md and the Security entry of this list on 30-r6-table say what `scripts/r6/ci.d/30-r6-table.sh` counts and refuses (routing run, ruling SG-34): a pin test that is weakened beside a changed pinned file fails the check, and one that is weakened alone does not; a second declaration of `PINNED` whose line ends after the name is not counted; and between an attribute and the declaration only blank lines and lines that start with `//`, `/*` or `*` count as comment lines. The script is unchanged.
- Added: The schema file of the interface run (interface run, U5.1, contracts.md I7.1 and I7.2, rulings IG-19, RD-5 and RD-6): the core list file registers the kind `coord_event`, family `coord`, bounded at 93 days and of origin own with the workflow id under the key `workflow`, and declares the error names `binding` and `seq`; `halvo_schema::coordinator` reads and writes the record `CoordEvent` with its nine events, the key `origin` of `item_answered` for the second step of a `sign_in` item and for a `link_account` item, the lapse causes `cancelled` of an item of a kind other than `confirm` and `check` of a `ready` item, and the fourteen ends of a stored `run_ended`, without `deleted`, which ends a run of a workflow that is gone and stores no row, and the coordinator bodies `coord_log` (`CoordLog`, reply `Stored`, at most 8 records and 4,096 bytes, counted on the input as it arrives before it is read and on the compact text when it is written) and `coord_history` (`CoordHistory`, with the same bound on the input, reply `History`, at most 64 records in ascending `seq`), each with a parser that refuses every other form with a `Refusal` that holds no value; the registry's `check_record` takes free text under `origin` for this kind, so a row is committed only after `CoordEvent::parse` or `CoordLog::parse` has read it; tests/golden/r4/ holds the records and the requests with their replies, and export-names writes the run's names into tests/golden/names.txt. The supervisor library halvo-agent-client (agent-setup run, U4.3, contracts.md A6.6, first pull request) starts a runner all or nothing under its mark in agents/marks/ of the data directory, as the leader of its process group on macOS and in a job of its own on Windows, relays its store requests over the owner link, answers its confirm, handback, bind, browser and secret frames through the caller's traits with add_origin after each act of the person, sends end_run for a runner that ended without a stored finish, and kills and reaps the processes of a run only while they still have the number and creation time of their mark.
- Added: Add the view of a person's agents, ui/app/views/agents.js, with its tests under ui/tests/r4/agents/: the list in the groups of the seven statuses, each row with its word, its line and its controls, and the card of one agent with its settings lines, its next day and its past runs, where each committed effect of a run stands as its own line with its kept fields as fields (`CardEffect` in `CardRun.effects` of halvo-coord, the words entries `run.effect.<class>`, `field.local_edit.file` and `field.local_edit.range`, and one committed payment in the state `card`). Add the view of what needs the person, `ui/app/views/needs.js`, which shows each open item of the coordinator's reply as a card, a confirmation of the page as a modal dialog, and sends only the calls of the command list, with its tests in `ui/tests/r4/needs/` and the fixture states `needs_confirm_mixed` and `needs_confirm_changed`, which show a confirmation of two classes of action and the line that something changed since the person said yes, and `docs/r4/page-features.txt` with the page features that the view uses.
- Changed: The words check 45-r4-words reads the sentence, the labels and the source words of each effect entry of a card's run in a fixture (contracts.md I1.5), with one failing fixture for each in its self-test. Change the page harness so that check (2) asks that a page confirmation takes the focus itself and that its safe control is the first stop of the walk key, reads a state that holds two confirmations, `act` reaches a control of an open confirmation with the walk key and Enter, a transcript's call line can hold its call or reject it, the stub keeps a line for each call that it rejects or cannot answer and each fixture that it cannot read (`trail(page)`), its waits include its own writes of `calls.jsonl` (`settled(page)`), and the self-test holds a sample state with two confirmations and its faults.
- Added: Add the gate checks of the service run, its memory, expiry, pause, delete-all, data-directory scan and content floor (sub-checks (f) to (k) and the scan of (b)), the fixture files of contracts C2.8 and C5.3, the bounds keys lease_bound_s, stop_deadline_s, prefill_events and expiry_bound_s, and the ci.d scripts 23-licences (cargo deny with deny.toml, npm audit), 24-hook-imports (the import tables of the Windows binaries) and 25-store-peak-rss (the 512 MiB store memory test).
- Changed: The timed-run line requires window_title and a second foreground_app event under input, run.sh prints the bounds blob in its first line and fails a line that a check prints under a name it does not own, the source scan reads block comments and fails an untied .post call, and the shell and workflow lints fail on another tool version than the pinned one.
- Fixed: The R1 gate checks refuse what they passed before: the data-directory scan reads names, UTF-16 and every output after the stop and lets a socket pass, expiry names the files of each expired day, delete-all reads whole names, rows and copies, a late helper window is ended, the licence check allows the owner's seven licences and denies an unused advisory ignore, the record is checked against the last gate commit, and the checks of gate-1 refuse a whole-line mismatch, zero rejected fixtures, a link below the store, a key after a carriage return, a hidden untracked directory, an odd source name, the e modifier and escaped workflow keys.
